*Yuxuan Ouyang,yingfeng luo,JingBo Zhu,Tong Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了BiasedTales-ML，一个大规模多语言儿童故事语料库，揭示了大语言模型在跨语言叙事生成中的差异，表明英语中心的评估不足以反映多语言社会背景下的生成行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对大语言模型的安全性和对齐性评估主要集中于英语，缺乏对多语言情境下叙事生成行为的系统研究，尤其忽视了低资源语言的表现。

Method: 构建了一个包含约35万条儿童故事的多语言平行语料库（BiasedTales-ML），覆盖八种类型和文化上多样的语言，采用全排列提示设计，并提出生成-提取管道与多维分布分析框架进行分析。

Result: 发现不同语言间叙事模式存在显著差异，英语中的分布特征难以迁移到其他语言，尤其在低资源语言中表现更明显；角色、场景和主题的结构模式在不同语言环境中呈现不同特征。

Conclusion: 英语中心的评估方法不足以全面衡量多语言环境下社会文化相关的叙事生成质量，需发展更具语言和文化包容性的评估体系。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate narrative content, including children's stories, which play an important role in social and cultural learning. Despite growing interest in AI safety and alignment, most existing evaluations focus primarily on English, leaving the cross-lingual generalization of aligned behavior underexplored. In this work, we introduce BiasedTales-ML, a large-scale parallel corpus of approximately 350,000 children's stories generated across eight typologically and culturally diverse languages using a full-permutation prompting design. We propose a structured generator-extractor pipeline and a multi-dimensional distributional analysis framework to examine how narrative attributes vary across languages, models, and social conditions. Our analysis reveals substantial cross-lingual variability in narrative generation patterns, indicating that distributions observed in English do not always exhibit similar characteristics in other languages, particularly in lower-resource settings. At the narrative level, we identify recurring structural patterns involving character roles, settings, and thematic emphasis, which manifest differently across linguistic contexts. These findings highlight the limitations of English-centric evaluation for characterizing socially grounded narrative generation in multilingual settings. We release the dataset, code, and an interactive visualization tool to support future research on multilingual narrative analysis and evaluation.

</details>


### [77] [Improving LLM Code Reasoning via Semantic Equivalence Self-Play with Formal Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17010)
*Antonio Valerio Miceli Barone,Poon Tsz Nok*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于Haskell语义等价的自博弈框架，结合形式化验证与对抗训练，并发布了一个包含约2.8万个验证程序的数据集OpInstruct-HSx。实验表明该方法在多个基准上显著提升性能，且等价性证明对模型推理能力至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了提升模型在Haskell程序语义等价判断上的推理能力，需要结合形式化验证与机器学习方法，解决现有方法缺乏严格验证信号的问题。

Method: 采用生成器-评估器的自博弈框架，利用Liquid Haskell进行等价性证明，执行反例检测不等价，并通过难度感知课程学习组织训练过程。

Result: 评估器在EquiBench上最高提升13.3个百分点，在PySecDB上也有持续增益；消融研究显示等价性证明对推理能力至关重要，而不等价监督主要增加数据量。

Conclusion: 形式化验证信号（如等价性证明）能有效增强模型的推理能力，所提出的框架和数据集为程序理解任务提供了可复现、可验证的新路径。

Abstract: We introduce a self-play framework for semantic equivalence in Haskell, utilizing formal verification to guide adversarial training between a generator and an evaluator. The framework leverages Liquid Haskell proofs for validating equivalence and execution-based counterexamples for inequivalence, organized via a difficulty-aware curriculum. To facilitate this, we release \textbf{OpInstruct-HSx}, a synthetic dataset of $\approx$28k validated Haskell programs. Empirical experiments show that our evaluator transfers effectively to downstream tasks, achieving up to 13.3pp accuracy gain on EquiBench and consistent gains on PySecDB. Ablation studies on the SEQ-SINQ regimes indicate that while inequivalence supervision provides data volume, equivalence proofs are uniquely responsible for the model's reasoning capabilities. The entire training pipeline and dataset are publicly released on GitHub and Hugging Face respectively.

</details>


### [78] [Beyond Black-Box Labels: Interpretable Criteria for Diagnosing SubjectiveNLP Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17022)
*Nisrine Rair,Alban Goupil,Valeriu Vrabie,Emmanuel Chochoy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种在确定金标签之前用于审计专家设计的标注模式的“模式级诊断”方法，利用多标注者的标准判断来识别主观NLP数据集中标注标准不稳定和类别边界模糊的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主观NLP数据集通常将标注者判断聚合为单一金标签，难以判断分歧是源于标准不明确、区分度不足还是合理的多样性。因此需要一种提前诊断标注模式缺陷的方法。

Method: 提出“模式级诊断”方法，利用多标注者的判别标准，在不依赖金标签的前提下，区分两种失败模式：标准不稳定（难以操作化）和类别间系统性重叠（互斥类别边界模糊）。

Result: 在商业文档的说服力价值提取任务中应用该方法，发现分歧并非广泛存在，而是集中在少数标准上，近一半被覆盖的句子触发多个类别，且这些信号与领域专家的分歧点一致。

Conclusion: 该诊断方法能有效识别标注模式中的问题，为改进标注指南、调整类别结构或重新考虑标注范式提供基于证据的审计支持。

Abstract: Subjective NLP datasets typically aggregate annotator judgments into a single gold label, making it difficult to diagnose whether disagreement reflects unclear criteria, collapsed distinctions, or legitimate plurality. We propose a \emph{schema-level diagnostic} for auditing expert-designed annotation schemas \emph{prior to} gold-label commitment, using only multi-annotator criterion judgments. The diagnostic separates two failure modes: unstable criteria with hard-to-operationalize boundaries, and systematic overlap that blurs the boundaries between mutually exclusive categories. Applied to persuasive value extraction in commercial documents, we find that disagreement is not diffuse: instability concentrates in a few criteria, while nearly half of covered sentences activate multiple categories. These signals align with where domain experts disagree, yielding an evidence-based audit for tightening guidelines, revising category structure, or reconsidering the annotation paradigm.

</details>


### [79] [Where is the Mind? Persona Vectors and LLM Individuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17031)
*Pierre Beckmann,Patrick Butlin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大语言模型的个体化问题，即哪些与模型相关的实体应被视为心智。作者通过机制可解释性方法，结合关于人格向量、人格空间和 emergent misalignment 的实证研究，提出三种可能的观点：虚拟实例观、虚拟实例-人格观和模型-人格观，并论证其合理性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大语言模型中‘谁’具有心智的问题，厘清模型运行过程中心理属性归属的本体论基础。

Method: 采用机制可解释性方法，分析注意力流、人格向量和人格空间等内部结构，结合三个关于人格内在结构的假设进行论证。

Result: 提出并论证了三种候选观点：虚拟实例视图认为注意力流在时间上维持准心理联系；两个新提出的基于人格的观点也显示出潜力。

Conclusion: 虚拟实例观及两种新人格观（虚拟实例-人格观和模型-人格观）是当前最具竞争力的解决方案，为理解大语言模型中的心智归属提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: The individuation problem for large language models asks which entities associated with them, if any, should be identified as minds. We approach this problem through mechanistic interpretability, engaging in particular with recent empirical work on persona vectors, persona space, and emergent misalignment. We argue that three views are the strongest candidates: the virtual instance view and two new views we introduce, the (virtual) instance-persona view and the model-persona view. First, we argue for the virtual instance view on the grounds that attention streams sustain quasi-psychological connections across token-time. Then we present the persona literature, organised around three hypotheses about the internal structure underlying personas in LLMs, and show that the two persona-based views are promising alternatives.

</details>


### [80] [Dynamic Emotion and Personality Profiling for Multimodal Deception Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17037)
*Li Zheng,Yanyi Luo,Hao Fei,Yuzhe Ding,Yujie Huang,Fei Li,Chong Teng,Donghong Ji*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种创新的多模型多提示标注方案和严格标签质量评估标准，构建了用于欺骗、情感和人格联合检测的多模态数据集DDEP，并提出了自适应可靠性加权融合框架Rel-DDEP，在三个任务上显著优于现有最先进基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法缺乏对情感和人格的样本级动态标注，限制了欺骗检测的效果，因此需要引入动态的情感与人格标注并设计有效的多模态融合机制。

Method: 提出多模型多提示标注方案和标签质量评估标准，构建DDEP数据集；设计Rel-DDEP框架，通过将模态特征映射到高维高斯分布空间量化不确定性，进行可靠性加权融合，并引入对齐模块和排序约束模块实现欺骗、情感与人格的联合检测。

Result: 在MDPE和DDEP数据集上的实验表明，所提方法在欺骗检测F1分数上提升2.53%，情感检测提升2.66%，人格检测提升9.30%。

Conclusion: 动态情感与人格标注对欺骗检测至关重要，可靠性加权融合策略有效提升了多模态联合检测性能。

Abstract: Deception detection is of great significance for ensuring information security and conducting public opinion analysis, with personality factors and emotion cues playing a critical role. However, existing methods lack sample-level dynamic annotations for emotions and personality.In this paper, we propose an innovative multi-model multi-prompt annotation scheme and a strict label quality evaluation standard, and establish a multimodal joint detection dataset DDEP for deception, emotion, and personality. Meanwhile, we propose Rel-DDEP, an adaptive reliability-weighted fusion framework. Our framework quantifies uncertainty by mapping modal features to a high-dimensional Gaussian distribution space. It then performs reliability-weighted fusion and incorporates an alignment module and a sorting constraint module to achieve joint detection of deception, emotion, and personality. Experimental results on the MDPE and DDEP datasets show that our Rel-DDEP significantly outperforms the existing state-of-the-art baseline models in three tasks. The F1 score of the deception detection increases by 2.53%, that of the emotion detection increases by 2.66%, and that of the personality detection increases by 9.30%. The experiments fully verify the necessity of annotating dynamic emotion and personality labels for each sample and the effectiveness of reliability-weighted fusion.

</details>


### [81] [Efficient Task Adaptation in Large Language Models via Selective Parameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17051)
*Weijie Wan,Jiangjiang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种参数元素重要性评估方法，通过区分参数在通用语言任务和特定领域任务中的重要性，将参数划分为“核心参数”和“非核心参数”，并在微调时固定核心参数，仅微调非核心参数，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘并提升了模型适应性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统微调方法在全参数空间进行训练，忽略了参数的异质性，导致预训练中获得的通用知识在特定领域微调时被覆盖或遗忘，限制了大语言模型的泛化与迁移能力。

Method: 提出一种参数元素重要性评估方法，根据参数对通用语言能力和特定任务的重要性进行划分，微调过程中保持核心参数不变，仅更新非核心参数。

Result: 在GPT-J和LLaMA-3上针对科学、医学和物理任务的大量实验表明，该方法能有效缓解灾难性遗忘，同时增强模型对特定任务的适应能力。

Conclusion: 通过区分并保护对通用能力至关重要的核心参数，仅微调非核心参数，能够在保持模型通用性的同时提升其在特定领域的性能，为高效微调提供了新思路。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated excellent performance in general language understanding, generation and other tasks. However, when fine-tuning for specific domain tasks, the general knowledge accumulated in the pre-training phase is often partially overwritten or forgotten due to parameter updates, which severely limits the generalization ability and transferability of LLMs. Traditional fine-tuning strategies mostly train on the entire parameter space, ignoring the heterogeneity of model parameters, that is, some parameters are extremely important for general tasks, while other parameters are more sensitive to specific tasks. To alleviate the above problems, this paper innovatively proposes a parameter element importance evaluation method, which divides parameters into "core parameters" and "non-core parameters" by distinguishing the importance of parameters for general language ability tasks and specific domain tasks, and fixes the core parameters during fine-tuning, and only fine-tunes the non-core parameters. Extensive experiments on scientific, medical and physical tasks using GPT-J and LLaMA-3 show that our method can mitigate catastrophic forgetting while enhancing the adaptability of the model.

</details>


### [82] [Jailbreaking Large Language Models with Morality Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17053)
*Ying Su,Mingen Zheng,Weili Diao,Haoran Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出利用越狱攻击来研究大语言模型（LLM）在多元价值观下的道德判断鲁棒性，构建了一个包含10.3K实例的道德数据集，并设计四种对抗攻击方法，实验表明LLM和防护模型在面对细微且复杂的道德攻击时存在严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有研究致力于提升大语言模型对道德多元主义的学习，但其在多元价值下生成道德内容的鲁棒性仍不清楚，因此需要探索模型在复杂道德情境下的脆弱性。

Method: 构建了一个包含‘价值模糊’和‘价值冲突’两类共10.3K个样本的道德数据集，并形式化了四种对抗性攻击方法，用于操纵LLM对道德问题的判断，评估了主流大语言模型和防护模型的表现。

Result: 实验结果显示，当前的大语言模型和防护模型在面对所提出的道德感知对抗攻击时表现出显著脆弱性，难以稳定维持一致的道德判断。

Conclusion: 研究表明，现有模型在实现真正意义上的多元主义对齐方面仍存在重大挑战，需进一步增强其在复杂道德情境下的鲁棒性和一致性。

Abstract: Pluralism alignment with AI has the sophisticated and necessary goal of creating AI that can coexist with and serve morally multifaceted humanity. Research towards pluralism alignment has many efforts in enhancing the learning of large language models (LLMs) to accomplish pluralism. Although this is essential, the robustness of LLMs to produce moral content over pluralistic values is still under exploration.Inspired by the astonishing persuasion abilities via jailbreak prompts, we propose to leverage jailbreak attacks to study LLMs' internal pluralistic values. In detail, we develop a morality dataset with 10.3K instances in two categories: Value Ambiguity and Value Conflict. We further formalize four adversarial attacks with the constructed dataset, to manipulate LLMs' judgment over the morality questions. We evaluate both the large language models and guardrail models which are typically used in generative systems with flexible user input. Our experiment results show that there is a critical vulnerability of LLMs and guardrail models to these subtle and sophisticated moral-aware attacks.

</details>


### [83] [Stability-Weighted Decoding for Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17068)
*Yue Wu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD)的新方法，用于改进扩散大语言模型（dLLMs）中的并行文本生成。该方法利用KL散度衡量连续预测分布之间的差异，以评估标记的时间不稳定性，并据此决定解码顺序，从而避免过早揭示不稳定标记。SWD无需训练，可作为通用调节器与任意基于分数的解码策略结合使用，在代码生成和数学推理任务中表现出更高的准确性和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的dLLM解码策略依赖于单步静态置信度量，忽略时间历史信息，容易导致不稳定标记被过早揭示，影响生成质量。为此，本文旨在通过引入时间稳定性机制来提升解码过程的安全性与准确性。

Method: 基于理论分析发现，标记的时间不稳定性（由连续预测分布间的KL散度量化）是其与剩余掩码上下文互信息的严格下界。因此，提出Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD)，将时间稳定性纳入标记评分过程，动态调整解码策略，适用于任何基于分数的解码政策，且无需额外训练。

Result: 在代码生成和数学推理基准上的实验表明，SWD在多种评分指标和选择策略下均能持续提升生成准确性，并在不同加速比下保持对基线方法的显著性能优势，展现出强健的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: SWD是一种有效、通用且无需训练的解码增强策略，通过利用时间稳定性信息提升了dLLMs的生成质量和可靠性，为并行文本生成提供了新的优化方向。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) enable parallel text generation by iteratively denoising a fully masked sequence, unmasking a subset of masked tokens at each step. Existing decoding strategies rely on static confidence metrics computed at a single denoising step, ignoring temporal history and often leading to premature unmasking of unstable tokens. In this work, we theoretically establish that a token's temporal instability, quantified by the KL divergence between consecutive prediction distributions, provides a strict lower bound on its mutual information with the remaining masked context, indicating that temporally unstable tokens are inherently unsafe to unmask. Based on this insight, we propose Stability-Weighted Decoding (SWD), a training-free, plug-and-play strategy that incorporates temporal stability into token scoring and acts as a universal modulator for arbitrary score-based decoding policies. Experiments on code generation and mathematical reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that SWD consistently improves generation accuracy across representative scoring metrics and selection policies, and exhibits exceptional robustness, maintaining a significant performance lead over standard baselines across varying acceleration ratios.

</details>


### [84] [Abstain-R1: Calibrated Abstention and Post-Refusal Clarification via Verifiable RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17073)
*Skylar Zhai,Jingcheng Liang,Dongyeop Kang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种澄清感知的强化学习奖励机制（clarification-aware RLVR reward），用于训练语言模型在面对无法回答的问题时既能主动 abstain（拒绝回答），又能提供语义对齐的澄清说明。通过该方法训练的Abstain-R1模型在多个基准上表现出优于基线模型的性能，且与更大规模的模型相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的回避回答方法往往生成泛化的拒绝或无效的澄清，缺乏对关键缺失信息的识别；作者希望构建一个不仅能拒绝回答，还能准确指出问题中缺失信息的语言模型。

Method: 提出一种新的强化学习奖励机制——RLVR奖励，该奖励在可回答问题上鼓励正确回答，在不可回答问题上联合优化显式拒绝行为和语义对齐的事后澄清。使用此奖励训练了一个3B参数的模型Abstain-R1。

Result: Abstain-R1在Abstain-Test、Abstain-QA和SelfAware等评测集上显著优于其基模型，并在处理不可回答问题时的表现与DeepSeek-R1等更大模型相媲美。

Conclusion: 研究表明，通过可验证的奖励信号可以有效学习到校准后的拒绝回答与澄清能力，而不仅仅依赖于模型规模的扩大。

Abstract: Reinforcement fine-tuning improves the reasoning ability of large language models, but it can also encourage them to answer unanswerable queries by guessing or hallucinating missing information. Existing abstention methods either train models to produce generic refusals or encourage follow-up clarifications without verifying whether those clarifications identify the key missing information. We study queries that are clear in meaning but cannot be reliably resolved from the given information, and argue that a reliable model should not only abstain, but also explain what is missing. We propose a clarification-aware RLVR reward that, while rewarding correct answers on answerable queries, jointly optimizes explicit abstention and semantically aligned post-refusal clarification on unanswerable queries. Using this reward, we train Abstain-R1, a 3B model that improves abstention and clarification on unanswerable queries while preserving strong performance on answerable ones. Experiments on Abstain-Test, Abstain-QA, and SelfAware show that Abstain-R1 substantially improves over its base model and achieves unanswerable-query behavior competitive with larger systems including DeepSeek-R1, suggesting that calibrated abstention and clarification can be learned through verifiable rewards rather than emerging from scale alone.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [BrainMem: Brain-Inspired Evolving Memory for Embodied Agent Task Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16331)
*Xiaoyu Ma,Lianyu Hu,Wenbing Tang,Zixuan Hu,Zeqin Liao,Zhizhen Wu,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: BrainMem是一种无需训练的分层记忆系统，受大脑启发，通过工作记忆、情景记忆和语义记忆提升具身智能体在复杂3D环境中的长视野任务规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于大语言模型的具身任务规划器缺乏持久记忆，导致重复错误且难以处理时空依赖问题，因此需要一种能持续积累经验的记忆机制。

Method: 提出BrainMem，将交互历史持续转化为结构化知识图谱和符号化指导规则，支持规划器检索和复用过往经验，无需模型微调或额外训练，并可与任意多模态大模型集成。

Result: 在四个代表性基准（EB-ALFRED、EB-Navigation等）上实验表明，BrainMem显著提升了不同模型和任务难度下的任务成功率，尤其在长视野和空间复杂任务中增益最大。

Conclusion: 演化记忆是一种有前景且可扩展的机制，有助于实现通用的具身智能。

Abstract: Embodied task planning requires agents to execute long-horizon, goal-directed actions in complex 3D environments, where success depends on both immediate perception and accumulated experience across tasks. However, most existing LLM-based planners are stateless and reactive, operating without persistent memory and therefore repeating errors and struggling with spatial or temporal dependencies. We propose BrainMem(Brain-Inspired Evolving Memory), a training-free hierarchical memory system that equips embodied agents with working, episodic, and semantic memory inspired by human cognition. BrainMem continuously transforms interaction histories into structured knowledge graphs and distilled symbolic guidelines, enabling planners to retrieve, reason over, and adapt behaviors from past experience without any model fine-tuning or additional training. This plug-and-play design integrates seamlessly with arbitrary multi-modal LLMs and greatly reduces reliance on task-specific prompt engineering. Extensive experiments on four representative benchmarks, including EB-ALFRED, EB-Navigation, EB-Manipulation, and EB-Habitat, demonstrate that BrainMem significantly enhances task success rates across diverse models and difficulty subsets, with the largest gains observed on long-horizon and spatially complex tasks. These results highlight evolving memory as a promising and scalable mechanism for generalizable embodied intelligence.

</details>


### [86] [Interdisciplinary Workshop on Mechanical Intelligence: Summary Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16381)
*Victoria A. Webster-Wood,Nicholas Gravish,Amir Alavi,Andres F Arrieta,Sarah Bergbreiter,Anthony Bloch,Laura Blumenschein,C. Chase Cao,Aja Mia Carter,Paolo Celli,Tony Chen,Margaret Coad,Mark Cutkosky,Michael Dickey,Brian Do,Robert Full,Mahdi Haghshenas-Jaryani,Kaushik Jayaram,Aaron Johnson,Eva Kanso,Emma Lejeune,Chen Li,Suyi Li,Jeffrey Lipton,Rob MacCurdy,Matt McHenry,Jean-Michel Mongeau,Todd Murphey,Mark Plecnik,Jordan Raney,Ryan D. Sochol,Hannah Stuart,Zeynep Temel,Michael Tolley,Barry Trimmer,T. J. Wallin,Kon-Well Wang,Wenzhong Yan,Mark Yim,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本报告总结了2024年机械智能跨学科研讨会的成果，探讨了材料、生物和机器人系统中机械结构本身如何通过响应性、适应性、记忆和学习来体现智能，区别于依赖电子信号和计算机代码的传统计算智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推动对机械智能（MI）这一新兴领域的理解，促进跨学科合作，探索不依赖传统计算方式的智能实现路径。

Method: 在NSF总部举行为期两天的研讨会，组织38位学术研究人员和8名NSF项目官员参与小组讨论，通过小组协作记录和最终汇报形式收集观点。

Result: 形成了关于机械智能的关键特征、研究挑战和发展方向的共识，并通过共享演示文稿汇总各组讨论结果。

Conclusion: 机械智能是一种通过物理结构实现智能行为的新范式，具有广阔的研究前景和应用潜力，需进一步加强跨学科协同研究。

Abstract: This report provides a summary of the outcomes of the Interdisciplinary Workshop on Mechanical Intelligence held in 2024. Mechanical Intelligence (MI) represents the phenomenon that novel structural features of material/biological/robotic systems can encode intelligence through responsiveness, adaptivity, memory, and learning in the mechanical structure itself. This is in contrast to computational intelligence, wherein the intelligence functions occur through electrical signaling and computer code. The two-day workshop was held at NSF headquarters on May 30-31 and included 38 invited academic researcher participants, and 8 program officers from the NSF. The workshop was structured around active small and large group discussions in groups of 4-5 and 9-10 with the goal of addressing topical questions on MI. Working groups entered notes into shared presentation slides for each discussion session and presented their outcomes in a final presentation on the last day. Here we summarize the overall outcomes of the workshop.

</details>


### [87] [RHINO-AR: An Augmented Reality Exhibit for Teaching Mobile Robotics Concepts in Museums](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16384)
*Nils Dengler,Tim Graf,Leif Van Holland,Patrick Stotko,Reinhard Klein,Maren Bennewitz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RHINO-AR是一个增强现实（AR）博物馆展品，将历史机器人RHINO的虚拟重建融入真实展览空间，提升观众对机器人导航技术的理解和体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决虚拟现实（VR）展览中观众与真实环境脱节的问题，弥合虚拟体验与物理展览空间之间的‘现实差距’。

Method: 基于RHINO-VR的工作，使用Magic Leap 2头显和Unity开发AR系统，结合实时环境网格化与LiDAR感知、可通行性分析和路径规划的交互式可视化。

Result: 在为期两天、22名参与者的研究中，RHINO-AR表现出良好的可用性和技术性能，有效传达了导航概念，且因更强的物理沉浸感和真实感而普遍优于VR版本。

Conclusion: AR技术能更有效地将复杂的机器人内部过程以直观方式呈现给非专业观众，同时保持与真实展览环境的连接，是博物馆科普展示的优选方案。

Abstract: We present RHINO-AR, an interactive Augmented Reality (AR) museum exhibit that reintroduces the historical mobile robot RHINO into its original exhibition environment at the Deutsches Museum Bonn. The system builds on our previous work RHINO-VR, which reconstructed the robot and the environment in virtual reality. Although this created an engaging experience, it also revealed an important limitation, because visitors were separated from the real exhibition space and from the physical robot on display. RHINO-AR addresses this reality gap by placing a virtual reconstruction of the robot directly into the real museum space. Implemented on a Magic Leap~2 headset using Unity, our system combines real-time environment meshing with interactive visualizations of LiDAR sensing, traversability, and path planning to make otherwise invisible robotics processes understandable to non-expert visitors. We evaluated RHINO-AR in a two-day museum study with 22 participants, assessing usability, technical performance, satisfaction, conceptual understanding, and preference comparison to RHINO-VR. The results show that RHINO-AR was well received, effectively conveyed key navigation concepts, and generally preferred over the VR exhibit due to its stronger physical grounding and increased realism.

</details>


### [88] [Visual-RRT: Finding Paths toward Visual-Goals via Differentiable Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16388)
*Sebin Lee,Jumin Lee,Taeyeon Kim,Younju Na,Woobin Im,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为视觉快速扩展随机树（vRRT）的运动规划方法，结合了基于梯度的利用和基于采样的探索，实现了基于视觉目标的机器人运动规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RRT规划器需要明确的目标构型，但在许多实际应用中，目标信息仅以图像或视频等视觉观察形式提供，难以获取精确的目标配置。因此，需要一种能够处理视觉目标的规划方法。

Method: 提出了vRRT方法，将可微分机器人渲染中的梯度利用与RRT的采样探索相结合，并引入了前沿驱动的探索-利用策略以及惯性梯度树扩展机制，以自适应地优先搜索有希望的视觉区域并保持动量一致的梯度利用。

Result: 在Franka、UR5e和Fetch等多种机器人操纵器上的大量实验表明，vRRT在模拟和真实世界环境中均能有效实现视觉目标规划。

Conclusion: vRRT成功弥合了基于采样的规划方法与以视觉为中心的机器人应用之间的差距，为视觉引导的机器人运动规划提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Rapidly-exploring random trees (RRTs) have been widely adopted for robot motion planning due to their robustness and theoretical guarantees. However, existing RRT-based planners require explicit goal configurations specified as numerical joint angles, while many practical applications provide goal specifications through visual observations such as images or demonstration videos where precise goal configurations are unavailable. In this paper, we propose visual-RRT (vRRT), a motion planner that enables visual-goal planning by unifying gradient-based exploitation from differentiable robot rendering with sampling-based exploration from RRTs. We further introduce (i) a frontier-based exploration-exploitation strategy that adaptively prioritizes visually promising search regions, and (ii) inertial gradient tree expansion that inherits optimization states across tree branches for momentum-consistent gradient exploitation. Extensive experiments across various robot manipulators including Franka, UR5e, and Fetch demonstrate that vRRT achieves effective visual-goal planning in both simulated and real-world settings, bridging the gap between sampling-based planning and vision-centric robot applications. Our code is available at https://sgvr.kaist.ac.kr/Visual-RRT.

</details>


### [89] [ICAT: Incident-Case-Grounded Adaptive Testing for Physical-Risk Prediction in Embodied World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16405)
*Zhenglin Lai,Sirui Huang,Yuteng Li,Changxin Huang,Jianqiang Li,Bingzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ICAT方法，基于真实事故报告和安全手册构建结构化风险记忆，用于评估视频生成型世界模型在预测物理风险和严重后果方面的能力，发现主流模型常忽略关键危险线索和严重结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成型世界模型在预测危险行为的严重后果时常忽略关键危险信号，影响其在安全关键场景中的可靠性，需系统性评估和改进其风险推理能力。

Method: 通过真实事故报告和安全手册构建结构化风险记忆，利用检索与组合机制（ICAT）约束风险案例生成，包含因果链和严重性标签，并建立基准进行评测。

Result: 实验表明主流世界模型常遗漏风险机制、触发条件，且对严重性估计不准，在ICAT基准上表现不佳。

Conclusion: 当前视频生成型世界模型在安全关键应用中可靠性不足，ICAT为提升其风险感知与因果推理提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Video-generative world models are increasingly used as neural simulators for embodied planning and policy learning, yet their ability to predict physical risk and severe consequences is rarely evaluated.We find that these models often downplay or omit key danger cues and severe outcomes for hazardous actions, which can induce unsafe preferences during planning and training on imagined rollouts. We propose ICAT, which grounds testing in real incident reports and safety manuals by building structured risk memories and retrieving/composing them to constrain the generation of risk cases with causal chains and severity labels. Experiments on an ICAT-based benchmark show that mainstream world models frequently miss mechanisms and triggering conditions and miscalibrate severity, falling short of the reliability required for safety-critical embodied deployment.

</details>


### [90] [LatentMimic: Terrain-Adaptive Locomotion via Latent Space Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16440)
*Zhiquan Wang,Yunyu Liu,Dipam Patel,Ayush Kumar,Aniket Bera,Bedrich Benes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为LatentMimic的新型运动学习框架，通过解耦风格保真度与几何约束，实现了四足机器人在复杂地形中的自适应运动控制，同时保持了较高的运动风格保真性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于模仿的方法在严格遵循动作捕捉参考与适应地形之间存在优化权衡，难以兼顾运动风格与地形适应能力。

Method: 提出LatentMimic框架，通过最小化策略状态-动作分布与学习到的动作捕捉先验之间的边际潜在散度，放松刚性的姿态跟踪目标；并引入带有动态回放缓冲区的地形适应模块以应对不同地形下的策略分布变化。

Result: 在四种运动风格和四种地形上验证了方法的有效性，相比现有最先进方法实现了更高的地形通过率，同时保持了高风格保真度。

Conclusion: LatentMimic能够在保持运动风格的同时实现对复杂地形的有效适应，解决了模仿学习中风格保真与地形适应之间的矛盾。

Abstract: Developing natural and diverse locomotion controllers for quadruped robots that can adapt to complex terrains while preserving motion style remains a significant challenge. Existing imitation-based methods face a fundamental optimization trade-off: strict adherence to motion capture (mocap) references penalizes the geometric deviations required for terrain adaptability, whereas terrain-centric policies often compromise stylistic fidelity. We introduce LatentMimic, a novel locomotion learning framework that decouples stylistic fidelity from geometric constraints. By minimizing the marginal latent divergence between the policy's state-action distribution and a learned mocap prior, our approach provides a conditional relaxation of rigid pose-tracking objectives. This formulation preserves gait topology while permitting independent end-effector adaptations for irregular terrains. We further introduce a terrain adaptation module with a dynamic replay buffer to resolve the policy's distribution shifts across different terrains. We validate our method across four locomotion styles and four terrains, demonstrating that LatentMimic enables effective terrain-adaptive locomotion, achieving higher terrain traversal success rates than state-of-the-art motion-tracking methods while maintaining high stylistic fidelity.

</details>


### [91] [Learning-Based Sparsification of Dynamic Graphs in Robotic Exploration Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16509)
*Adithya V. Sastry,Bibek Poudel,Weizi Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Transformer和PPO的强化学习框架，用于在机器人探索过程中动态剪枝图结构，有效减少96%的图规模，提升探索一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图的机器人探索算法因图结构快速增长、积累冗余信息而导致性能下降，亟需有效的图剪枝方法。

Method: 设计了一个基于Transformer的框架，并使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练，以在探索过程中智能地剪枝动态图结构。

Result: 在RRT模拟的前端探索任务中，该方法最多可将图大小减少96%，尽管探索速率略低于基线，但标准差最低，表现出更稳定的跨环境探索能力。

Conclusion: 实验结果初步证明，强化学习可用于机器人探索中动态图的稀疏化，且具备实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Many robotic exploration algorithms rely on graph structures for frontier-based exploration and dynamic path planning. However, these graphs grow rapidly, accumulating redundant information and impacting performance. We present a transformer-based framework trained with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) to prune these graphs during exploration, limiting their growth and reducing the accumulation of excess information. The framework was evaluated on simulations of a robotic agent using Rapidly Exploring Random Trees (RRT) to carry out frontier-based exploration, where the learned policy reduces graph size by up to 96%. We find preliminary evidence that our framework learns to associate pruning decisions with exploration outcomes despite sparse, delayed reward signals. We also observe that while intelligent pruning achieves a lower rate of exploration compared to baselines, it yields the lowest standard deviation, producing the most consistent exploration across varied environments. To the best of our knowledge, these results are the first suggesting the viability of RL in sparsification of dynamic graphs used in robotic exploration algorithms.

</details>


### [92] [Emergency Stopping for Liquid-manipulating Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16667)
*Samuli Hynninen,Ville Kyrki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于机器人操作开放容器中液体的紧急停止系统，通过模型预测控制框架生成时间最优且防溅的停止轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在意外危险情况下，传统方法难以避免因急停导致的液体飞溅，因此需要一种能够快速响应并防止液体溢出的紧急停止机制。

Method: 将紧急停止问题建模为最优控制问题，并在模型预测控制框架下求解，生成时间最优且不溢出的停止轨迹，作为现有运动规划方法之上的即插即用安全层。

Result: 通过仿真和在7-DoF Franka Emika Panda机器人上的实验验证，该方法能够在检测到危险时实现快速、无溅射的紧急停止。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能有效提升机器人操作液体时的安全性，适用于需应急响应的应用场景。

Abstract: Manipulating open liquid containers is challenging because liquids are highly sensitive to vessel accelerations and jerks. Although spill-free liquid manipulation has been widely studied, emergency stopping under unexpected hazards has received little attention, despite the fact that abrupt braking may cause hazardous spills. This letter presents an emergency stop system for robots manipulating liquids in open containers. We formulate emergency stopping as an optimal control problem and solve it in a model predictive control framework to generate time-optimal, spill-free stopping trajectories. The method operates as a plug-and-play safety layer on top of existing slosh-free motion planning methods, enabling immediate reaction to detected hazards while accounting for nonlinear liquid dynamics. We demonstrate, through simulation and on a 7-DoF Franka Emika Panda robot, that the proposed approach achieves fast emergency stopping without spilling.

</details>


### [93] [Diffusion-Based Optimization for Accelerated Convergence of Redundant Dual-Arm Minimum Time Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16670)
*Jushan Chen,Jonathan Fried,Santiago Paternain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于扩散的框架，利用概率采样解决双臂机器人非凸高层优化问题，显著减少了运行时间和笛卡尔误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了解决先前工作中梯度法计算开销大以及难以直接施加L∞笛卡尔误差约束的问题。

Method: 采用基于模型的扩散算法新变体，结合概率采样来处理非凸高层问题。

Result: 相比之前的工作，运行时间减少了35倍，笛卡尔误差降低了34%。

Conclusion: 所提出的扩散框架在减少计算开销和提高轨迹跟踪精度方面优于之前的梯度法方法。

Abstract: We present a framework leveraging a novel variant of the model-based diffusion algorithm to minimize the time required for a redundant dual-arm robot configuration to follow a desired relative Cartesian path. Our prior work proposed a bi-level optimization approach for the dual-arm problem, where we derived the analytical solution to the lower-level convex sub-problem and solved the high-level nonconvex problem using a primal-dual approach. However, the gradient-based nature leads to a large computation overhead, and it prohibits directly imposing an $L_{\infty}$ Cartesian error constraint along the joint trajectory due to the sparsity of the gradient. In this work, we propose a diffusion-based framework that relies on probabilistic sampling to tackle the aforementioned challenges in the nonconvex high-level problem, leading to a 35x reduction in the runtime and 34\% less Cartesian error compared to our prior work.

</details>


### [94] [ReconVLA: An Uncertainty-Guided and Failure-Aware Vision-Language-Action Framework for Robotic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16677)
*Lingling Chen,Zongyao Lyu,William J. Beksi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReconVLA是一种可靠且符合规范的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，通过将符合性预测应用于预训练VLA的动作输出，提供校准的不确定性估计和故障预警能力，无需重新训练即可提升控制可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的VLA模型缺乏对动作预测的置信度量化，限制了其在现实场景中的可靠性；因此需要引入可校准的不确定性评估机制以提前识别失败风险。

Method: 将符合性预测直接应用于预训练VLA模型的动作标记输出，并扩展至机器人状态空间以检测异常或不安全状态，从而实现动作级和状态级的双重不确定性感知与故障检测。

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人实验中，ReconVLA显著提升了失败预判能力，减少了灾难性错误，并提供了与执行质量及任务成功率相关的校准置信度。

Conclusion: ReconVLA无需重新训练即可为现有VLA模型增强可靠性，通过校准的不确定性估计和前置式故障检测机制，有效提升了通用机器人控制器在复杂任务中的安全性与实用性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models have emerged as generalist robotic controllers capable of mapping visual observations and natural language instructions to continuous action sequences. However, VLAs provide no calibrated measure of confidence in their action predictions, thus limiting their reliability in real-world settings where uncertainty and failures must be anticipated. To address this problem we introduce ReconVLA, a reliable conformal model that produces uncertainty-guided and failure-aware control signals. Concretely, our approach applies conformal prediction directly to the action token outputs of pretrained VLA policies, yielding calibrated uncertainty estimates that correlate with execution quality and task success. Furthermore, we extend conformal prediction to the robot state space to detect outliers or unsafe states before failures occur, providing a simple yet effective failure detection mechanism that complements the action-level uncertainty. We evaluate ReconVLA in both simulation and real robot experiments across diverse manipulation tasks. Our results show that conformalized action predictions consistently improve failure anticipation, reduce catastrophic errors, and provide a calibrated measure of confidence without retraining or modifying the underlying VLA.

</details>


### [95] [Rewind-IL: Online Failure Detection and State Respawning for Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16683)
*Gehan Zheng,Sanjay Seenivasan,Matthew Johnson-Roberson,Weiming Zhi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Rewind-IL的无需训练的在线保护框架，用于生成式动作块模仿策略，通过零样本失败检测和状态重置机制提升机器人在长周期操作任务中的执行可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习虽能赋予机器人复杂的视觉运动操控能力，但在长周期任务中常因执行偏离演示轨迹而导致失败，且现有监控方法依赖故障数据或缺乏恢复机制，限制了实际部署的可靠性。

Method: 提出Rewind-IL框架，结合基于时间块间差异估计（TIDE）与分裂保形预测校准的零样本失败检测器，以及基于视觉语言模型识别恢复检查点的状态重生机制；离线构建紧凑的检查点特征数据库，在线监测动作块间的自洽性与检查点相似性，并在检测到失败时回滚至最近的安全状态并重启策略推断。

Result: 在真实世界和模拟的长周期操作任务中实验表明，Rewind-IL有效提升了策略执行的可靠性，并可迁移应用于flow-matching动作块策略，验证了其通用性和实用性。

Conclusion: 将策略内部一致性与语义锚定的重生机制相结合，为模仿学习提供了无需训练、实用且可靠的运行时安全保障路径。

Abstract: Imitation learning has enabled robots to acquire complex visuomotor manipulation skills from demonstrations, but deployment failures remain a major obstacle, especially for long-horizon action-chunked policies. Once execution drifts off the demonstration manifold, these policies often continue producing locally plausible actions without recovering from the failure. Existing runtime monitors either require failure data, over-trigger under benign feature drift, or stop at failure detection without providing a recovery mechanism. We present Rewind-IL, a training-free online safeguard framework for generative action-chunked imitation policies. Rewind-IL combines a zero-shot failure detector based on Temporal Inter-chunk Discrepancy Estimate (TIDE), calibrated with split conformal prediction, with a state-respawning mechanism that returns the robot to a semantically verified safe intermediate state. Offline, a vision-language model identifies recovery checkpoints in demonstrations, and the frozen policy encoder is used to construct a compact checkpoint feature database. Online, Rewind-IL monitors self-consistency in overlapping action chunks, tracks similarity to the checkpoint library, and, upon failure, rewinds execution to the latest verified safe state before restarting inference from a clean policy state. Experiments on real-world and simulated long-horizon manipulation tasks, including transfer to flow-matching action-chunked policies, demonstrate that policy-internal consistency coupled with semantically grounded respawning offers a practical route to improved reliability in imitation learning. Supplemental materials are available at https://sjay05.github.io/rewind-il

</details>


### [96] [Autonomous Vehicle Collision Avoidance With Racing Parameterized Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16702)
*Shathushan Sivashangaran,Vihaan Dutta,Apoorva Khairnar,Sepideh Gohari,Azim Eskandarian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于深度强化学习（DRL）的自动驾驶车辆碰撞避免策略，能够在无显式轨迹引导的情况下，在仿真和硬件上实现高效、安全的避障，显著优于传统MPC-APF方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于人类驾驶错误导致大量交通事故，自动驾驶车辆有潜力大幅提升道路安全，但需在复杂动态环境中实现高精度且高效的紧急避障。

Method: 采用深度强化学习（DRL）训练碰撞避免策略，利用赛车超车场景进行Out-Of-Distribution训练，引入物理信息感知和模拟器漏洞感知的奖励函数，编码非线性车辆动力学，评估了正向与反向行驶两种策略。

Result: DRL策略在三种交叉路口碰撞场景中均优于MPC-APF基线方法，计算量减少31倍，推理延迟降低64倍；反向行驶策略在正面碰撞中比默认策略优30%，比基线优50%，在侧碰中表现相当，且两种DRL策略均比数值最优控制多避免10%的碰撞。

Conclusion: 所提出的DRL碰撞避免策略在保证高安全性的同时，具备出色的计算效率和零样本迁移能力，适用于真实硬件部署，为自动驾驶在复杂交通环境中的应急响应提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Road traffic accidents are a leading cause of fatalities worldwide. In the US, human error causes 94% of crashes, resulting in excess of 7,000 pedestrian fatalities and $500 billion in costs annually. Autonomous Vehicles (AVs) with emergency collision avoidance systems that operate at the limits of vehicle dynamics at a high frequency, a dual constraint of nonlinear kinodynamic accuracy and computational efficiency, further enhance safety benefits during adverse weather and cybersecurity breaches, and to evade dangerous human driving when AVs and human drivers share roads. This paper parameterizes a Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) collision avoidance policy Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) utilizing race car overtaking, without explicit geometric mimicry reference trajectory guidance, in simulation, with a physics-informed, simulator exploit-aware reward to encode nonlinear vehicle kinodynamics. Two policies are evaluated, a default uni-direction and a reversed heading variant that navigates in the opposite direction to other cars, which both consistently outperform a Model Predictive Control and Artificial Potential Function (MPC-APF) baseline, with zero-shot transfer to proportionally scaled hardware, across three intersection collision scenarios, at 31x fewer Floating Point Operations (FLOPS) and 64x lower inference latency. The reversed heading policy outperforms the default racing overtaking policy in head-to-head collisions by 30% and the baseline by 50%, and matches the former in side collisions, where both DRL policies evade 10% greater than numerical optimal control.

</details>


### [97] [LiDAR-based Crowd Navigation with Visible Edge Group Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16741)
*Allan Wang,Aaron Steinfeld*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于可见边的群体表示方法，用于机器人在密集人群中的导航，通过简化群体表征，在保证安全性和社交性的同时提升了计算速度，并在真实机器人上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的行人群体检测方法在高密度人群场景中受限于外部检测模块的噪声和遮挡问题，且多局限于低密度环境，难以实际部署。因此，需要一种更鲁棒、高效的群体表征方法以提升机器人在复杂人群中的导航性能。

Method: 提出了一种“基于可见边的群体表示”方法，不依赖于对个体的精确跟踪，而是利用可见的局部结构信息来推断群体关系，并将其集成到导航框架中，通过仿真和真实机器人实验验证其性能。

Result: 实验表明，群体预测精度在高密度环境中对导航性能影响较小；所提方法在密集人群下实现了与现有方法相当的安全性和社交性，同时显著提高了计算速度。

Conclusion: 基于可见边的群体表示是一种适用于高密度人群环境的轻量且鲁棒的导航方法，有利于群体感知在实际机器人系统中的部署。

Abstract: Robot navigation in crowded pedestrian environments is a well-known challenge and we explore the practical deployment of group-based representations in this setting. Pedestrian groups have been empirically shown to enable a mobile robot's navigation behavior to be safer and more social. However, existing approaches either explored groups only in limited scenarios with no high-density crowds or depended on external detection modules to track individuals, which are prone to noise and errors due to occlusions in crowds. We show that group prediction accuracy affects navigation performance only marginally in crowded environments. Based on this observation, we propose the visible edge-based group representation. We additionally demonstrate via simulation experiments that our navigation framework, integrated with the simplified group representation, performs comparatively in terms of safety and socialness in dense crowds, while achieving faster computation speed. Finally, we deploy our navigation framework on a real robot to explore the benefits of practically deploying group-based representations in the real world.

</details>


### [98] [LongBench: Evaluating Robotic Manipulation Policies on Real-World Long-Horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16788)
*Xueyao Chen,Jingkai Jia,Tong Yang,Yibo Fu,Wei Li,Wenqiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了LongBench，一个用于评估真实世界长视野操作任务的基准测试，包含超过1000个实际场景，涵盖完全可观测和依赖上下文两种模式，支持对执行鲁棒性、时间一致性和上下文推理能力的细粒度分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作策略在长视野任务中表现下降，但已有基准测试难以揭示失败原因，尤其是缺乏对真实世界中不同时间难度来源的分解分析。

Method: 构建了一个名为LongBench的真实世界基准，包含1000多个真实操作片段，分为上下文无关（完全可观测）和上下文相关（歧义驱动）两类，并按能力和歧义类型组织任务子集，以实现机制感知的评估。

Result: 对六种最先进策略的评估表明，长视野性能不受单一因素主导；在完全可观测环境中，执行鲁棒性影响更大，而上下文难度因任务而异，基于记忆的方法并未一致提升表现。

Conclusion: LongBench为研究长视野操作提供了有价值的工具，有助于开发在执行和上下文挑战下更具鲁棒性的策略。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation policies often degrade over extended horizons, yet existing benchmarks provide limited insight into why such failures occur. Most prior benchmarks are either simulation-based or report aggregate success, making it difficult to disentangle the distinct sources of temporal difficulty in real-world execution. We introduce LongBench, a real-world benchmark for evaluating long-horizon manipulation. LongBench consists of over 1,000 real-world episodes, covering two complementary regimes: Context-Independent (fully observable) and Context-Dependent (ambiguity-driven). By organizing tasks into capability- and ambiguity-specific subsets, LongBench enables mechanism-aware evaluation of execution robustness, temporal consistency, and context-dependent reasoning. Evaluating six state-of-the-art policies reveals that long-horizon performance is not governed by a single factor. We observe that performance in fully observable settings is more strongly associated with execution robustness, while contextual difficulty varies across tasks and is not consistently improved by memory-based methods. We hope that LongBench serves as a useful benchmark for studying long-horizon manipulation and for developing policies with stronger robustness across both execution and contextual challenges.

</details>


### [99] [Refinement of Accelerated Demonstrations via Incremental Iterative Reference Learning Control for Fast Contact-Rich Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16850)
*Koki Yamane,Cristian C. Beltran-Hernandez,Steven Oh,Masashi Hamaya,Sho Sakaino*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种渐进式迭代参考学习控制（I2RLC）方法，用于自主优化高速接触丰富操作中的演示轨迹，显著提升轨迹精度和稳定性，并成功应用于模仿学习，实现比原始演示更快且高成功率的策略训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在接触丰富的操作任务中实现快速执行对实际部署至关重要，但通过人类演示获取高速动作为模仿学习提供数据存在困难：人类无法高速演示，而直接加速演示会改变接触动力学并导致较大的跟踪误差。因此需要一种能自主优化高速演示轨迹的方法。

Method: 重新利用迭代参考学习控制（IRLC）框架，从观测到的跟踪误差中迭代更新参考轨迹；进一步提出增量式IRLC（I2RLC），通过逐步提高速度的同时更新参考轨迹，以减小早期迭代误差并提升瞬态稳定性。在白板擦除和插 peg-in-hole 任务中使用遥操作实验验证方法有效性，并将优化后的轨迹用于训练模仿学习策略。

Result: IRLC 和 I2RLC 均可实现最高10倍速的演示且降低跟踪误差；I2RLC 在三个任务和多个速度下平均比 IRLC 提高22.5%的空间相似性；基于I2RLC优化轨迹训练的IL策略在peg-in-hole任务中达到100%成功率（包括未见位置），且接触力更低。

Conclusion: 结合渐进式速度提升与参考轨迹自适应更新的I2RLC为实现快速、高保真、接触丰富的模仿学习提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Fast execution of contact-rich manipulation is critical for practical deployment, yet providing fast demonstrations for imitation learning (IL) remains challenging: humans cannot demonstrate at high speed, and naively accelerating demonstrations alters contact dynamics and induces large tracking errors. We present a method to autonomously refine time-accelerated demonstrations by repurposing Iterative Reference Learning Control (IRLC) to iteratively update the reference trajectory from observed tracking errors. However, applying IRLC directly at high speed tends to produce larger early-iteration errors and less stable transients. To address this issue, we propose Incremental Iterative Reference Learning Control (I2RLC), which gradually increases the speed while updating the reference, yielding high-fidelity trajectories. We validate on real-robot whiteboard erasing and peg-in-hole tasks using a teleoperation setup with a compliance-controlled follower and a 3D-printed haptic leader. Both IRLC and I2RLC achieve up to 10x faster demonstrations with reduced tracking error; moreover, I2RLC improves spatial similarity to the original trajectories by 22.5% on average over IRLC across three tasks and multiple speeds (3x-10x). We then use the refined trajectories to train IL policies; the resulting policies execute faster than the demonstrations and achieve 100% success rates in the peg-in-hole task at both seen and unseen positions, with I2RLC-trained policies exhibiting lower contact forces than those trained on IRLC-refined demonstrations. These results indicate that gradual speed scheduling coupled with reference adaptation provides a practical path to fast, contact-rich IL.

</details>


### [100] [Greedy Kalman-Swarm: Improving State Estimation in Robot Swarms in Harsh Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16868)
*Phunyapa Suksomboon,Paulo Garcia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为“Greedy Kalman-Swarm”的局部化分布式状态估计方法，可在通信受限环境中提升机器人集群的状态估计精度，兼具低开销与高鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卡尔曼滤波依赖集中式处理或高通信开销，难以在实际部署的机器人集群中实现高效全局状态估计。

Method: 采用局部‘贪婪’策略，利用机器人间的相对感知信息，在无需全局通信的情况下实时融合邻居数据以优化个体状态估计。

Result: 仿真表明该方法在通信受限环境下仍能有效提升估计精度，并保持系统鲁棒性，性能介于独立估计与集中式系统之间。

Conclusion: 全局状态感知可通过去中心化机制涌现，该方法为复杂环境中可扩展、强韧的自主集群系统提供了新框架。

Abstract: State estimation is a fundamental requirement in robotics, where the accurate determination of a robot's state is essential for stable operation despite inherent process disturbances and sensor noise. Traditionally, this is achieved through Kalman filtering, providing a statistically optimal estimate by balancing predictive models with noisy measurements. In the context of robotic swarms, the challenge shifts from individual accuracy to collective coordination, where the integration of global dynamics can significantly enhance the precision of the entire group. Existing estimation techniques rely on centralized processing or heavy communication protocols to reach a global consensus, which are frequently impractical in real-world deployments. Here we show that a localized, "greedy" approach to distributed state estimation (termed "Greedy Kalman-Swarm") allows individual robots to leverage relative inter-robot sensing for improved accuracy without requiring full data availability or global communication. Simulations in communication-constrained environments show robots can effectively integrate all currently available neighbor data at each iteration to refine their internal states, yet remain robust and functional even when data is missing. This results in a performance profile that strikes a balance between the low overhead of independent estimation and the high accuracy of centralized systems, specifically under harsh or dynamic environmental conditions. Our results demonstrate that global state awareness can be emergent rather than enforced, providing a scalable framework for maintaining swarm cohesion in unpredictable terrains. We anticipate that this decentralized methodology will serve as a foundation for more resilient autonomous systems, particularly in search-and-rescue or space exploration missions where reliable, high-bandwidth communication cannot be guaranteed.

</details>


### [101] [Chain Of Interaction Benchmark (COIN): When Reasoning meets Embodied Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16886)
*Xianhao Wang,Xiaojian Ma,Haozhe Hu,Rongpeng Su,Yutian Cheng,Zhou Ziheng,Hangxin Liu,Lei Liu,Bin Li,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了COIN基准，用于评估现实场景中机器人操作的交互式推理能力，包含50个日常任务、低成本移动AR遥操作系统收集的数据集以及系统性评估指标，揭示了当前模型在视觉理解与运动执行之间存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用具身智能体需要在部分可观测环境下进行连续交互、信息获取和计划更新以解决长视野任务，但现有基准未能系统评估这一关键能力。

Method: 构建COIN-50任务集，设计因果依赖任务所需的COIN-Primitive和中等复杂度的COIN-Composition；开发低成本移动AR遥操作系统并收集包含1000个演示的COIN-Primitive数据集；提出针对执行稳定性和泛化鲁棒性的系统评估指标。

Result: 通过评估CodeAsPolicy、VLA和语言条件H-VLA方法，发现当前模型在交互式推理任务上表现不佳，主要受限于视觉理解与运动执行之间的巨大鸿沟。

Conclusion: COIN为评估具身智能体的交互式推理提供了新的基准，突出了未来研究需弥合感知与行动之间差距的重要性。

Abstract: Generalist embodied agents must perform interactive, causally-dependent reasoning, continually interacting with the environment, acquiring information, and updating plans to solve long-horizon tasks before they could be adopted in real-life scenarios. For instance, retrieving an apple from a cabinet may require opening multiple doors and drawers before the apple becomes visible and reachable, demanding sequential interaction under partial observability. However, existing benchmarks fail to systematically evaluate this essential capability. We introduce COIN, a benchmark designed to assess interactive reasoning in realistic robotic manipulation through three key contributions. First, we construct COIN-50: 50 interactive tasks in daily scenarios, and create COIN-Primitive required by causally-dependent tasks, and COIN-Composition with mid-term complexity for skill learning and generalization evaluation. Second, we develop a low-cost mobile AR teleoperation system and collect the COIN-Primitive Dataset with 50 demonstrations per primitive task (1,000 in total). Third, we develop systematic evaluation metrics about execution stability and generalization robustness to evaluate CodeAsPolicy, VLA, and language-conditioned H-VLA approaches. Our comprehensive evaluation reveals critical limitations in current methods: models struggle with interactive reasoning tasks due to significant gaps between visual understanding and motor execution. We provide fine-grained analysis of these limitations.

</details>


### [102] [Time-Division Multiplexing Actuation in Tendon-Driven Arms: Lightweight Design and Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16887)
*Shoujie Li,Changqing Guo,Jianle Xu,Hong Luo,Xueqian Wang,Wenbo Ding,Bin Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于腱驱动机器人的时分复用驱动（TDMA）方法，通过创新的硬件设计和控制算法，在减少驱动器数量的同时实现了轻量化、高扭矩输出和固有容错能力，验证了MuxArm在多种环境下的鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航空航天应用中的机械臂需要在轻量化与容错操作之间取得平衡，以满足严格的重量限制并确保在远程危险环境中可靠运行。传统方法难以兼顾驱动器数量、重量与容错性，因此需要一种新的驱动架构来解决这一矛盾。

Method: 提出时分复用驱动（TDMA）技术，采用垂直堆叠的旋转选择结构，集成自旋转TDM电机、电磁离合器、蜗轮减速器和双编码器系统；开发了驱动空间轨迹规划算法，实现容错控制并降低肌腱负载。

Result: MuxArm自重2.17公斤，驱动能力达10公斤，末端执行器精度保持在其长度的1%以内，即使在部分伺服失效情况下仍能正常工作；实验表明肌腱负载可降低50%，并在自由空间、杂乱和受限环境中展现出强健性能。

Conclusion: TDMA为腱驱动机器人提供了一种实用且高效的解决方案，能够在显著减少驱动器数量的同时保持高性能和容错能力，适用于对重量和可靠性要求严苛的航空航天任务。

Abstract: Robotic manipulators for aerospace applications require a delicate balance between lightweight construction and fault-tolerant operation to satisfy strict weight limitations and ensure reliability in remote, hazardous environments. This paper presents Time-Division Multiplexing Actuation (TDMA), a practical approach for tendon-driven robots that significantly reduces actuator count while preserving high torque output and intrinsic fault tolerance. The key hardware employs a vertically-stacked rotational selection structure that integrates self-rotating TDM motors for rapid configuration, electromagnetic clutches enabling sub-0.1 second engagement, a worm gear reducer for enhanced load capacity and self-locking capability, and a dual-encoder system for precise, long-term positioning. Leveraging TDMA, the proposed MuxArm achieves a self-weight of 2.17 kg, supports an actuator driving capacity of 10 kg, and maintains end-effector accuracy up to 1% of its length, even under partial servo failure. Additionally, an actuation space trajectory planning algorithm is developed, enabling fault-tolerant control and reducing tendon load by up to 50% compared to conventional methods. Comprehensive experiments demonstrate MuxArm's robust performance in diverse settings, including free-space, cluttered, and confined environments.

</details>


### [103] [Leveraging VR Robot Games to Facilitate Data Collection for Embodied Intelligence Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16903)
*Yihan Zhang,Ziyun Huang,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于Unity的虚拟现实游戏化数据收集框架，用于低成本、可扩展地收集具身交互数据，并通过拾取垃圾的任务验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于传统接口的访问受限，大规模收集具身交互数据成本高且困难，因此需要一种更高效、可扩展的数据收集方法。

Method: 开发了一个基于Unity的游戏化数据收集框架，结合程序化场景生成、VR控制的人形机器人操作、自动任务评估和轨迹记录，以实现高效的数据采集。

Result: 实验结果表明，所收集的示范数据覆盖了广泛的状态-动作空间，任务难度增加会提升运动强度并扩大机械臂工作空间的探索范围。

Conclusion: 该框架证明面向游戏的虚拟环境可以作为具身数据收集的有效且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Collecting embodied interaction data at scale remains costly and difficult due to the limited accessibility of conventional interfaces. We present a gamified data collection framework based on Unity that combines procedural scene generation, VR-based humanoid robot control, automatic task evaluation, and trajectory logging. A trash pick-and-place task prototype is developed to validate the full workflow.Experimental results indicate that the collected demonstrations exhibit broad coverage of the state-action space, and that increasing task difficulty leads to higher motion intensity as well as more extensive exploration of the arm's workspace. The proposed framework demonstrates that game-oriented virtual environments can serve as an effective and extensible solution for embodied data collection.

</details>


### [104] [NaviFormer: A Deep Reinforcement Learning Transformer-like Model to Holistically Solve the Navigation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16967)
*Daniel Fuertes,Andrea Cavallaro,Carlos R. del-Blanco,Fernando Jaureguizar,Narciso García*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer架构的深度强化学习模型NaviFormer，用于同时解决全局导航中的高层路径规划和低层轨迹预测问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法通常将路径规划分为高层路线规划和低层轨迹生成两个子问题分别处理，难以满足现实场景中对整体性和实时性的需求。

Method: 提出NaviFormer模型，采用Transformer架构结合深度强化学习，统一预测高層路线与低层轨迹。

Result: 实验表明，NaviFormer在准确率上具有竞争力，并展现出优越的计算速度，适合实时任务。

Conclusion: NaviFormer能够有效理解和应对两类子问题的约束与挑战，实现高效、协同的全局导航规划。

Abstract: Path planning is usually solved by addressing either the (high-level) route planning problem (waypoint sequencing to achieve the final goal) or the (low-level) path planning problem (trajectory prediction between two waypoints avoiding collisions). However, real-world problems usually require simultaneous solutions to the route and path planning subproblems with a holistic and efficient approach. In this paper, we introduce NaviFormer, a deep reinforcement learning model based on a Transformer architecture that solves the global navigation problem by predicting both high-level routes and low-level trajectories. To evaluate NaviFormer, several experiments have been conducted, including comparisons with other algorithms. Results show competitive accuracy from NaviFormer since it can understand the constraints and difficulties of each subproblem and act consequently to improve performance. Moreover, its superior computation speed proves its suitability for real-time missions.

</details>


### [105] [Web-Gewu: A Browser-Based Interactive Playground for Robot Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17050)
*Kaixuan Chen,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Web-Gewu，一个基于WebRTC云-边-端协同架构的交互式机器人教育平台，通过将物理仿真和强化学习训练卸载到边缘节点，实现低成本、低延迟的浏览器端实时交互，降低具身智能教育的技术门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有机器人教育中计算资源门槛高、环境配置复杂的问题，尤其是集中式云仿真成本高和本地计算受硬件限制的困境。

Method: 构建基于WebRTC的云-边-端协同架构，边缘节点负责物理仿真和强化学习训练，云服务器仅作为轻量信令中继，实现浏览器端对多形态机器人的P2P实时流交互，并集成多维监控数据可视化。

Result: 实现了无需本地安装、跨设备低延迟交互的机器人教学平台，支持强化学习奖励曲线等实时可视化，具备高可扩展性和即用性。

Conclusion: Web-Gewu显著降低了具身智能技术的学习与教学门槛，为大规模机器人教育提供了高效、低成本且易于部署的解决方案。

Abstract: With the rapid development of embodied intelligence, robotics education faces a dual challenge: high computational barriers and cumbersome environment configuration. Existing centralized cloud simulation solutions incur substantial GPU and bandwidth costs that preclude large-scale deployment, while pure local computing is severely constrained by learners' hardware limitations. To address these issues, we propose \href{http://47.76.242.88:8080/receiver/index.html}{Web-Gewu}, an interactive robotics education platform built on a WebRTC cloud-edge-client collaborative architecture. The system offloads all physics simulation and reinforcement learning (RL) training to the edge node, while the cloud server acts exclusively as a lightweight signaling relay, enabling extremely low-cost browser-based peer-to-peer (P2P) real-time streaming. Learners can interact with multi-form robots at low end-to-end latency directly in a web browser without any local installation, and simultaneously observe real-time visualization of multi-dimensional monitoring data, including reinforcement learning reward curves. Combined with a predefined robust command communication protocol, Web-Gewu provides a highly scalable, out-of-the-box, and barrier-free teaching infrastructure for embodied intelligence, significantly lowering the barrier to entry for cutting-edge robotics technology.

</details>


### [106] [Modeling, Control and Self-sensing of Dielectric Elastomer Soft Actuators: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17199)
*Y. Zhao,G. Meng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了介电弹性体驱动器（DEA）在软体机器人中的应用，重点讨论了其建模、控制与自感知技术的研究进展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于DEA存在非线性弹性、粘弹性蠕变、滞后和振动等特性，导致其建模、控制和自感知困难，限制了实际应用，因此需要系统总结现有方法并指明未来方向。

Method: 对基于物理和现象学的建模方法、多种控制策略（开环前馈、反馈、前馈-反馈及自适应前馈控制）以及物理模型和数据驱动的自感知方法进行了分类综述。

Result: 总结了当前DEA在建模、控制与自感知方面的主流方法，分析了各类方法的优缺点，并梳理了该领域存在的问题和发展机遇。

Conclusion: 尽管DEA具有优异性能，但其复杂动态特性仍带来挑战；未来需进一步发展高精度建模、鲁棒控制与无传感器自感知技术以推动其广泛应用。

Abstract: Dielectric elastomer actuators (DEAs) have garnered extensive attention especially in soft robotic applications over the past few decades owing to the advantages of lightweight, large strain, fast response and high energy density. However, because the DEAs suffer from nonlinear elasticity, inherent viscoelastic creep, hysteresis and vibrational dynamics, the modeling, control and self-sensing of DEAs are challenging, thereby hindering the practical applications of DEAs. In order to address these challenges, numerous studies have been conducted. In this review, various physics-based modeling methods and phenomenological modeling methods for predicting the electromechanical response of DEAs are presented and discussed. Different control methods for DEAs are reviewed, which are classified into open-loop feedforward control, feedback control, feedforward-feedback control and adaptive feedforward control. Physics-based self-sensing methods and data-driven self-sensing methods for reconstructing the DEA displacement without the need for additional sensors are discussed. Finally, the existing problems and new opportunities for the further studies are summarized.

</details>


### [107] [Planning Smooth and Safe Control Laws for a Unicycle Robot Among Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17212)
*Aref Amiri,Basak Sakcak,Steven M. LaValle*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种用于单轮机器人在障碍物环境中安全导航的框架，通过光滑向量场和非线性反馈控制器实现快速、低控制代价的安全路径跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在存在障碍物且具有输入限制的环境下，实现单轮机器人从几乎任意初始状态到目标点的安全导航，同时保证系统的安全性与收敛性。

Method: 引入一种新的QP公式以生成C∞光滑的向量场，并设计了解析的非线性反馈控制器，该控制器天然满足Nagumo定理条件，确保安全集的前向不变性，无需在线优化。

Result: 在硬性输入限制下，控制器能够安全地收敛到目标位置；仿真结果显示到达时间快了两倍，角控制 effort 降低超过50%。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架能够在保证安全的前提下，显著提升导航效率并降低控制消耗，适用于对实时性和安全性要求高的场景。

Abstract: This paper presents a framework for safe navigation of a unicycle point robot to a goal position in an environment populated with obstacles from almost any admissible state, considering input limits. We introduce a novel QP formulation to create a Cinfinity-smooth vector field with reduced total bending and total turning. Then we design an analytic, non-linear feedback controller that inherently satisfies the conditions of Nagumo's theorem, ensuring forward invariance of the safe set without requiring any online optimization. We have demonstrated that our controller, even under hard input limits, safely converges to the goal position. Simulations confirm the effectiveness of the proposed framework, resulting in a twice faster arrival time with over 50\% lower angular control effort compared to the baseline.

</details>


### [108] [GaLa: Hypergraph-Guided Visual Language Models for Procedural Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17241)
*Kun Wang,Yiming Li,Mingcheng Qu,Aqiang Zhang,Guang Yang,Tonghua Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为GaLa的视觉语言框架，用于多模态程序规划，通过超图表示和三重视图编码器提升对复杂场景中功能空间关系的理解能力，并在多个基准上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法过度依赖视觉语言模型自身的推理能力，忽视了多模态输入中可挖掘的丰富结构化语义信息，导致在理解复杂场景中的功能空间关系时表现不佳。

Method: 提出GaLa框架，采用基于超图的表示方法，将图像中的物体实例建模为节点，并根据属性和功能语义聚合构建区域级超边；设计三重视图超图编码器（TriView HyperGraph Encoder），通过对比学习在节点视图、区域视图和节点-区域关联视图之间强制语义一致性，从而更有效地将超图语义注入下游VLM推理中。

Result: 在ActPlan1K和ALFRED基准上的大量实验表明，GaLa在执行成功率、LCS和规划正确性方面显著优于现有方法。

Conclusion: GaLa通过显式建模隐式空间关系和深层语义结构，有效提升了多模态程序规划的性能，验证了利用结构化语义信息增强VLM推理的有效性。

Abstract: Implicit spatial relations and deep semantic structures encoded in object attributes are crucial for procedural planning in embodied AI systems. However, existing approaches often over rely on the reasoning capabilities of vision language models (VLMs) themselves, while overlooking the rich structured semantic information that can be mined from multimodal inputs. As a result, models struggle to effectively understand functional spatial relationships in complex scenes. To fully exploit implicit spatial relations and deep semantic structures in multimodal data, we propose GaLa, a vision language framework for multimodal procedural planning. GaLa introduces a hypergraph-based representation, where object instances in the image are modeled as nodes, and region-level hyperedges are constructed by aggregating objects according to their attributes and functional semantics. This design explicitly captures implicit semantic relations among objects as well as the hierarchical organization of functional regions. Furthermore, we design a TriView HyperGraph Encoder that enforces semantic consistency across the node view, area view, and node area association view via contrastive learning, enabling hypergraph semantics to be more effectively injected into downstream VLM reasoning. Extensive experiments on the ActPlan1K and ALFRED benchmarks demonstrate that GaLa significantly outperforms existing methods in terms of execution success rate, LCS, and planning correctness.

</details>


### [109] [MM-Hand: A 21-DOF Multi-modal Modular Dexterous Robotic Hand with Remote Actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17245)
*Zhuoheng Li,Qingquan Lin,Checheng Yu,Qiangyu Chen,Zhiqian Lan,Lutong Zhang,Hongyang Li,Ping Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于远程肌腱驱动的21自由度多模态模块化灵巧手MM-Hand，集成了传感、轻量化和可维护设计，并开源了全部软硬件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统高自由度灵巧手存在空间占用大、重量高和散热差的问题，需通过创新设计提升性能与实用性。

Method: 采用远程肌腱驱动将电机移至基座或外置电机 hub，结合弹簧回位肌腱结构、模块化3D打印部件、快速肌腱连接器及多模态感知系统（关节角度、触觉、电机反馈和掌内立体视觉），并分析肌腱鞘长度变化与摩擦损耗以优化传动与控制。

Result: 实验验证了系统的传动性能、输出力（1米远距离下指尖力达25N）、感知与闭环关节控制能力，在静态与运动状态下均表现出良好的指令跟踪性能。

Conclusion: MM-Hand提供了一个轻量、高集成度、可维护且传感器丰富的灵巧操作研究平台，推动灵巧手在实际应用中的发展。

Abstract: High-DOF dexterous hands require compact actuation, rich sensing, and reliable thermal behavior, but conventional designs often occupy valuable in-hand space, increase end-effector mass, and suffer from heat accumulation near the hand. Remote tendon-driven actuation offers an alternative by relocating motors to the robot base or an external motor hub, thereby freeing the fingers and palm for additional degrees of freedom, sensing modules, and maintainable mechanical structures. This paper presents MM-Hand, a 21-DOF Multimodal Modular dexterous hand based on remote tendon-driven actuation. The hand integrates spring-return tendon-driven fingers, modular 3D-printed finger and palm structures, quick tendon connectors for maintenance, and a multimodal sensing system including joint angle sensors, tactile sensors, motor-side feedback, and in-palm stereo vision. We further analyze tendon-sheath length variation and friction loss to guide the design of the routing, motor hub, and closed-loop joint control. Experiments validate the transmission, output force, sensing, and control capability of the system. The fingertip force reaches 25N under a 1m remote sheath transmission, demonstrating practical load capacity despite long-distance tendon routing. Closed-loop joint-level experiments further evaluate command tracking with a static arm and during arm motion. These results show that MM-Hand provides a lightweight, sensor-rich, and maintainable hardware platform for dexterous manipulation research. To support the community, all hardware designs and software frameworks are made fully open-source at https://mmlab.hk/research/MM-Hand.

</details>


### [110] [A Rapid Deployment Pipeline for Autonomous Humanoid Grasping Based on Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17258)
*Yifei Yan,Yankai Liao,Linqi Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种端到端的快速部署流程，利用三种基础模型组件将人形机器人操作新物体的上线时间从一两天缩短至约30分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统部署方式耗时长，需要大量人工干预和专用设备，限制了人形机器人在实际场景中的灵活应用。

Method: 该方法结合基于Roboflow的自动标注、Meta SAM 3D实现的3D重建，以及直接使用SAM 3D生成网格作为模板的FoundationPose进行零样本6自由度姿态跟踪，并驱动Unity逆运动学规划器通过UDP向Unitree G1机器人发送指令。

Result: 实现了mAP@0.5=0.995的检测精度、σ<1.05mm的姿态追踪精度，并在真实机器人上完成了工作空间内五个位置的成功抓取；在汽车车窗涂胶任务中验证了流程的通用性。

Conclusion: 结合基础模型与日常成像设备（如智能手机）可显著降低人形机器人操作任务的部署门槛，提升部署效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Deploying a humanoid robot to manipulate a new object has traditionally required one to two days of effort: data collection, manual annotation, 3D model acquisition, and model training. This paper presents an end-to-end rapid deployment pipeline that integrates three foundation-model components to shorten the onboarding cycle for a new object to approximately 30 minutes: (i) Roboflow-based automatic annotation to assist in training a YOLOv8 object detector; (ii) 3D reconstruction based on Meta SAM 3D, which eliminates the need for a dedicated laser scanner; and (iii) zero-shot 6-DoF pose tracking based on FoundationPose, using the SAM~3D-generated mesh directly as the template. The estimated pose drives a Unity-based inverse kinematics planner, whose joint commands are streamed via UDP to a Unitree~G1 humanoid and executed through the Unitree SDK. We demonstrate detection accuracy of mAP@0.5 = 0.995, pose tracking precision of $σ< 1.05$ mm, and successful grasping on a real robot at five positions within the workspace. We further verify the generality of the pipeline on an automobile-window glue-application task. The results show that combining foundation models for perception with everyday imaging devices (e.g., smartphones) can substantially lower the deployment barrier for humanoid manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [111] [Learning Whole-Body Humanoid Locomotion via Motion Generation and Motion Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17335)
*Zewei Zhang,Kehan Wen,Michael Xu,Junzhe He,Chenhao Li,Takahiro Miki,Clemens Schwarke,Chong Zhang,Xue Bin Peng,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合参考动作学习与地形感知自适应的全身人形机器人运动框架，通过扩散模型实时预测地形感知的参考动作，并利用强化学习训练全身动作跟踪器，实现了在复杂地形上的稳定行走。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在人形机器人全身运动控制中存在局限：基于奖励塑造的强化学习容易导致下肢主导行为，而基于模仿学习的方法难以在线适应不同地形。需要一种能够结合参考动作并实时根据感知信息进行自适应调整的全身运动控制方法。

Method: 首先在重定向的人类动作数据上训练一个扩散模型，用于实时预测地形感知的参考动作；同时使用这些动作用强化学习训练一个全身参考跟踪器；为进一步提升在参考生成不完美情况下的鲁棒性，在闭环设置下用冻结的运动生成器对跟踪器进行微调。

Result: 该系统支持带方向目标到达控制和地形感知的全身自适应能力，可在配备机载感知与计算的Unitree G1人形机器人上部署；硬件实验成功穿越了箱子、障碍物、楼梯及混合地形；定量结果表明，引入在线动作生成和对动作跟踪器的微调有助于提升泛化性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效结合了模仿学习与在线地形感知自适应，实现了鲁棒且灵活的全身人形机器人运动控制，能够在复杂真实环境中稳定行走，具备良好的部署能力和应用前景。

Abstract: Whole-body humanoid locomotion is challenging due to high-dimensional control, morphological instability, and the need for real-time adaptation to various terrains using onboard perception. Directly applying reinforcement learning (RL) with reward shaping to humanoid locomotion often leads to lower-body-dominated behaviors, whereas imitation-based RL can learn more coordinated whole-body skills but is typically limited to replaying reference motions without a mechanism to adapt them online from perception for terrain-aware locomotion. To address this gap, we propose a whole-body humanoid locomotion framework that combines skills learned from reference motions with terrain-aware adaptation. We first train a diffusion model on retargeted human motions for real-time prediction of terrain-aware reference motions. Concurrently, we train a whole-body reference tracker with RL using this motion data. To improve robustness under imperfectly generated references, we further fine-tune the tracker with a frozen motion generator in a closed-loop setting. The resulting system supports directional goal-reaching control with terrain-aware whole-body adaptation, and can be deployed on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot with onboard perception and computation. The hardware experiments demonstrate successful traversal over boxes, hurdles, stairs, and mixed terrain combinations. Quantitative results further show the benefits of incorporating online motion generation and fine-tuning the motion tracker for improved generalization and robustness.

</details>


### [112] [Think before Go: Hierarchical Reasoning for Image-goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17407)
*Pengna Li,Kangyi Wu,Shaoqing Xu,Fang Li,Lin Zhao,Long Chen,Zhi-Xin Yang,Nanning Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于图像目标导航的分层推理框架HRNav，通过将任务分解为高层规划和低层执行，并引入视觉语言模型与强化学习策略结合的方法，有效缓解了长距离导航中的徘徊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端方法在目标遥远或位于不同房间时难以提取有效视觉线索，导致智能体徘徊。受人类认知中高级推理引导低级执行的启发，本文旨在通过分层结构提升导航性能。

Method: 提出HRNav框架：高层使用训练好的视觉语言模型生成短视距计划（如是否穿过门或走廊）；低层采用在线强化学习策略根据该计划决策动作，并设计了抑制徘徊的惩罚机制（WSP）。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，所提方法显著优于现有方法，有效减少了徘徊现象，提升了长距离图像目标导航的成功率。

Conclusion: HRNav通过分层设计实现了更高效的图像目标导航，验证了将高级语义规划与低级动作控制相结合的有效性，为复杂环境下的具身智能任务提供了新思路。

Abstract: Image-goal navigation steers an agent to a target location specified by an image in unseen environments. Existing methods primarily handle this task by learning an end-to-end navigation policy, which compares the similarities of target and observation images and directly predicts the actions. However, when the target is distant or lies in another room, such methods fail to extract informative visual cues, leading the agent to wander around. Motivated by the human cognitive principle that deliberate, high-level reasoning guides fast, reactive execution in complex tasks, we propose Hierarchical Reasoning Navigation (HRNav), a framework that decomposes image-goal navigation into high-level planning and low-level execution. In high-level planning, a vision-language model is trained on a self-collected dataset to generate a short-horizon plan, such as whether the agent should walk through the door or down the hallway. This downgrades the difficulty of the long-horizon task, making it more amenable to the execution part. In low-level execution, an online reinforcement learning policy is utilized to decide actions conditioned on the short-horizon plan. We also devise a novel Wandering Suppression Penalty (WSP) to further reduce the wandering problem. Together, these components form a hierarchical framework for Image-Goal Navigation. Extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate the superiority of our method.

</details>


### [113] [FLASH: Fast Learning via GPU-Accelerated Simulation for High-Fidelity Deformable Manipulation in Minutes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17513)
*Siyuan Luo,Bingyang Zhou,Chong Zhang,Xin Liu,Zhenhao Huang,Gang Yang,Zhengtao Han,Xiaotian Hu,Eric Yang,Rymon Yu,Ziqiu Zeng,Fan Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FLASH是一个基于GPU原生架构的仿真框架，专为接触密集型可变形物体操作设计，能够实现高精度、高速度和稳定的模拟，并支持从仿真到现实的零样本迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的仿真框架在处理可变形物体时面临精度、速度和稳定性不足的问题，尤其是在需要大量自由度和复杂接触约束的场景下，限制了机器人学习在可变形物体操作中的应用。

Method: 提出FLASH框架，采用基于NCP的求解器并从底层重新设计物理引擎，充分利用现代GPU架构进行细粒度并行计算，优化碰撞检测和内存布局，以实现高效且准确的可变形体模拟。

Result: 在单个RTX 5090上实现了超过300万自由度的模拟，达到30 FPS；仅使用FLASH生成的合成数据训练的策略可在无真实世界演示的情况下成功迁移到实际机器人完成毛巾折叠、衣物折叠等复杂任务。

Conclusion: FLASH为可变形物体的机器人操作提供了一个高效、精确且可扩展的仿真解决方案，显著降低了对真实世界数据收集的依赖，推动了sim-to-real在变形物体操作中的实际应用。

Abstract: Simulation frameworks such as Isaac Sim have enabled scalable robot learning for locomotion and rigid-body manipulation; however, contact-rich simulation remains a major bottleneck for deformable object manipulation. The continuously changing geometry of soft materials, together with large numbers of vertices and contact constraints, makes it difficult to achieve high accuracy, speed, and stability required for large-scale interactive learning. We present FLASH, a GPU-native simulation framework for contact-rich deformable manipulation, built on an accurate NCP-based solver that enforces strict contact and deformation constraints while being explicitly designed for fine-grained GPU parallelism. Rather than porting conventional single-instruction-multiple-data (SIMD) solvers to GPUs, FLASH redesigns the physics engine from the ground up to leverage modern GPU architectures, including optimized collision handling and memory layouts. As a result, FLASH scales to over 3 million degrees of freedom at 30 FPS on a single RTX 5090, while accurately simulating physical interactions. Policies trained solely on FLASH-generated synthetic data in minutes achieve robust zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, which we validate on physical robots performing challenging deformable manipulation tasks such as towel folding and garment folding, without any real-world demonstration, providing a practical alternative to labor-intensive real-world data collection.

</details>


### [114] [Safer Trajectory Planning with CBF-guided Diffusion Model for Unmanned Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17527)
*Peiwen Yang,Shiyu Bai,Weisong Wen,Yixin Gao,Jiahao Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩散模型的无人机轨迹生成框架AeroTrajGen，结合控制屏障函数（CBF）引导采样，在保证安全性的同时实现高敏捷性飞行轨迹生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对扩散模型在轨迹生成中存在不可预测性和安全隐患的问题，以及对大量安全验证训练数据的依赖，提出引入CBF引导采样机制以提升生成轨迹的安全性与可靠性。

Method: 设计了一个融合控制屏障函数引导采样的扩散模型，在反向扩散过程中将安全约束梯度与扩散模型得分函数结合；采用障碍物感知的扩散Transformer架构，并支持多模态条件输入（如历史轨迹、障碍物、动作风格和目标）。

Result: 在包含多障碍环境的仿真中评估，相比无引导的扩散模型基线，CBF引导采样使碰撞率降低94.7%，同时保持了轨迹的敏捷性和多样性。

Conclusion: AeroTrajGen通过引入CBF引导采样，有效平衡了生成轨迹的安全性与敏捷性，减少了对安全训练数据的依赖，为无人机复杂机动提供了可靠且高效的轨迹规划方案。

Abstract: Safe and agile trajectory planning is essential for autonomous systems, especially during complex aerobatic maneuvers. Motivated by the recent success of diffusion models in generative tasks, this paper introduces AeroTrajGen, a novel framework for diffusion-based trajectory generation that incorporates control barrier function (CBF)-guided sampling during inference, specifically designed for unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). The proposed CBF-guided sampling addresses two critical challenges: (1) mitigating the inherent unpredictability and potential safety violations of diffusion models, and (2) reducing reliance on extensively safety-verified training data. During the reverse diffusion process, CBF-based guidance ensures collision-free trajectories by seamlessly integrating safety constraint gradients with the diffusion model's score function. The model features an obstacle-aware diffusion transformer architecture with multi-modal conditioning, including trajectory history, obstacles, maneuver styles, and goal, enabling the generation of smooth, highly agile trajectories across 14 distinct aerobatic maneuvers. Trained on a dataset of 2,000 expert demonstrations, AeroTrajGen is rigorously evaluated in simulation under multi-obstacle environments. Simulation results demonstrate that CBF-guided sampling reduces collision rates by 94.7% compared to unguided diffusion baselines, while preserving trajectory agility and diversity. Our code is open-sourced at https://github.com/RoboticsPolyu/CBF-DMP.

</details>


### [115] [Novel Algorithms for Smoothly Differentiable and Efficiently Vectorizable Contact Manifold Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17538)
*Onur Beker,Andreas René Geist,Anselm Paulus,Georg Martius*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种在接触丰富的环境中生成智能机器人行为的可微分碰撞检测方法，通过可微的SDF基元和新的接触流形生成算法实现高效、可扩展的仿真。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零阶方法在接触丰富的机器人行为生成中占主导地位，但利用一阶或二阶动力学信息有望提升求解速度与计算效率，而获取有效梯度的主要瓶颈在于仿真流程中碰撞检测、接触动力学和时间积分的不可微问题。

Method: 提出了一个可微且高度向量化的方法来解决碰撞检测的可微性问题，包括：1）一类能高效表示复杂3D表面的解析SDF基元；2）基于该几何表示的新型接触流形生成算法。

Result: 实现了对碰撞检测过程的平滑可微分处理，支持高效的梯度计算，并具备良好的向量化能力，为端到端可微物理仿真提供了关键组件。

Conclusion: 该方法解决了接触仿真中碰撞检测环节的可微性难题，为利用梯度信息优化机器人控制策略奠定了基础，推动了高效率、高精度仿真学习的发展。

Abstract: Generating intelligent robot behavior in contact-rich settings is a research problem where zeroth-order methods currently prevail. Developing methods that make use of first/second order information about the dynamics holds great promise in terms of increasing the solution speed and computational efficiency. The main bottleneck in this research direction is the difficulty in obtaining useful gradients and Hessians, due to pathologies in all three steps of a common simulation pipeline: i) collision detection, ii) contact dynamics, iii) time integration. This abstract proposes a method that can address the collision detection part of the puzzle in a manner that is smoothly differentiable and massively vectorizable. This is achieved via two contributions: i) a highly expressive class of analytical SDF primitives that can efficiently represent complex 3D surfaces, ii) a novel contact manifold generation routine that makes use of this geometry representation.

</details>


### [116] [A Hamilton-Jacobi Reachability-Guided Search Framework for Efficient and Safe Indoor Planar Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.17679)
*Hanyang Hu,Cameron Siu,Mo Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合离线Hamilton-Jacobi（HJ）可达性分析与在线图搜索的自主导航规划方法，通过利用预计算的HJ值函数作为启发式信息和安全约束，提升了在线规划的效率与安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的图搜索算法在高维空间中计算复杂度高，难以实现实时规划；而HJ可达性分析虽能提供安全保证，但需要环境的完整先验知识，限制了其在动态环境中的应用。
